23:T47a,<div class="lab-head"><h4>The product rule is an area picture</h4><span class="tag">Lab 4 · grow the rectangle</span></div>
<canvas aria-label="Rectangle of width u and height v with growth strips" class="tall"></canvas>
<div class="controls">
<label for="pr-x">x <input id="pr-x" max="2" min="0.6" step="0.01" type="range" value="1.4"/></label>
<label for="pr-dx">Δx <input id="pr-dx" max="0.6" min="0" step="0.005" type="range" value="0.35"/></label>
</div>
<div class="readout">
<div>u = x (width), v = x² (height)<b id="pr-uv">–</b></div>
<div class="c-f">u·Δv (top strip)<b id="pr-a">–</b></div>
<div class="c-d">v·Δu (side strip)<b id="pr-b">–</b></div>
<div class="c-t">Δu·Δv (corner)<b id="pr-c">–</b></div>
<div>Δ(uv) / Δx<b id="pr-q">–</b></div>
<div>u v′ + v u′ = x·2x + x²·1<b id="pr-d">–</b></div>
</div>
<p class="note">The area uv grows by two strips plus a tiny corner: Δ(uv) = u·Δv + v·Δu + Δu·Δv. Divide by Δx and let Δx → 0. The corner is a product of two small numbers, so it disappears, leaving (uv)′ = u v′ + v u′. Drag Δx toward 0 and watch the two bottom numbers meet.</p>18:["$","$L21","1",{"children":["$","$L22",null,{"id":"lab-prod","html":"$23"}]}]
19:["$","$L21","2",{"children":["$","h3",null,{"id":"$undefined","children":"Worked examples"}]}]
